\u003ccode\u003epnpm\u003c/code\u003e for source checkouts. The repository is a pnpm workspace, and bundled\nplugins load from \u003ccode\u003eextensions/*\u003c/code\u003e during development so their package-local\ndependencies and your edits are used directly. Plain \u003ccode\u003enpm install\u003c/code\u003e at the repo\nroot is not a supported source setup.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eFor the dev loop:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"git clone https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw.git\ncd openclaw\n\npnpm install\n\n# First run only (or after resetting local OpenClaw config/workspace)\npnpm openclaw setup\n\n# Optional: prebuild Control UI before first startup\npnpm ui:build\n\n# Dev loop (auto-reload on source/config changes)\npnpm gateway:watch\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003egit clone https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw.git\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003ecd\u003c/span\u003e openclaw\n\npnpm install\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e First run only (or after resetting local OpenClaw config/workspace)\u003c/span\u003e\npnpm openclaw setup\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Optional: prebuild Control UI before first startup\u003c/span\u003e\npnpm ui:build\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Dev loop (auto-reload on source/config changes)\u003c/span\u003e\npnpm gateway:watch\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eIf you need a built \u003ccode\u003edist/\u003c/code\u003e from the checkout (for Node, packaging, or release validation), run:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"pnpm build\npnpm ui:build\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003epnpm build\npnpm ui:build\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003epnpm openclaw setup\u003c/code\u003e writes the local config/workspace needed for \u003ccode\u003epnpm gateway:watch\u003c/code\u003e. It is safe to re-run, but you normally only need it on first setup or after resetting local state. \u003ccode\u003epnpm gateway:watch\u003c/code\u003e does not rebuild \u003ccode\u003edist/control-ui\u003c/code\u003e, so rerun \u003ccode\u003epnpm ui:build\u003c/code\u003e after \u003ccode\u003eui/\u003c/code\u003e changes or use \u003ccode\u003epnpm ui:dev\u003c/code\u003e when iterating on the Control UI. If you want this checkout to run onboarding directly, use \u003ccode\u003epnpm openclaw onboard --install-daemon\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eNote: \u003ccode\u003epnpm openclaw ...\u003c/code\u003e runs TypeScript directly (via \u003ccode\u003etsx\u003c/code\u003e). \u003ccode\u003epnpm build\u003c/code\u003e produces \u003ccode\u003edist/\u003c/code\u003e for running via Node / the packaged \u003ccode\u003eopenclaw\u003c/code\u003e binary, while \u003ccode\u003epnpm gateway:watch\u003c/code\u003e rebuilds the runtime on demand during the dev </p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>OpenClaw</strong> is a <em>personal AI assistant</em> you run on your own devices.
It answers you on the channels you already use. It can speak and listen on macOS/iOS/Android, and can render a live Canvas you control. The Gateway is just the control plane — the product is the assistant.</p>
<p dir="auto">If you want a personal, single-user assistant that feels local, fast, and always-on, this is it.</p>
<p dir="auto">Supported channels include: WhatsApp, Telegram, Slack, Discord, Google Chat, Signal, iMessage, IRC, Microsoft Teams, Matrix, Feishu, LINE, Mattermost, Nextcloud Talk, Nostr, Synology Chat, Tlon, Twitch, Zalo, Zalo Personal, WeChat, QQ, WebChat.</p>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://openclaw.ai" rel="nofollow">Website</a> · <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai" rel="nofollow">Docs</a> · <a href="/openclaw/openclaw/blob/main/VISION.md">Vision</a> · <a href="/openclaw/openclaw/blob/main/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md">Third-party notices</a> · <a href="https://deepwiki.com/openclaw/openclaw" rel="nofollow">DeepWiki</a> · <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/start/getting-started" rel="nofollow">Getting Started</a> · <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating" rel="nofollow">Updating</a> · <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/start/showcase" rel="nofollow">Showcase</a> · <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/help/faq" rel="nofollow">FAQ</a> · <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/start/wizard" rel="nofollow">Onboarding</a> · <a href="https://github.com/openclaw/nix-openclaw">Nix</a> · <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/docker" rel="nofollow">Docker</a> · <a href="https://discord.gg/clawd" rel="nofollow">Discord</a></p>
<p dir="auto">New install? Start here: <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/start/getting-started" rel="nofollow">Getting started</a></p>
<p dir="auto">Preferred setup: run <code>openclaw onboard</code> in your terminal.
OpenClaw Onboard guides you step by step through setting up the gateway, workspace, channels, and skills. It is the recommended CLI setup path and works on <strong>macOS, Linux, and Windows</strong>.
Windows desktop users can start with the native <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/platforms/windows" rel="nofollow">Windows Hub</a> companion app for setup, tray status, chat, node mode, and local MCP mode.
Works with npm, pnpm, or bun.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Quick start (TL;DR)</h2><a id="user-content-quick-start-tldr" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Quick start (TL;DR)" href="#quick-start-tldr"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Runtime: <strong>Node 24 (recommended) or Node 22.19+</strong>.</p>
<p dir="auto">Full beginner guide (auth, pairing, channels): <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/start/getting-started" rel="nofollow">Getting started</a></p>
<p dir="auto">Recommended daemon mode:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="openclaw onboard --install-daemon
openclaw gateway status"><pre>openclaw onboard --install-daemon
openclaw gateway status</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Foreground/debug mode:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="openclaw gateway stop
openclaw gateway --port 18789 --verbose"><pre>openclaw gateway stop
openclaw gateway --port 18789 --verbose</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Send a test message or ask the assistant after either startup mode is running:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Send a message
openclaw message send --target +1234567890 --message &quot;Hello from OpenClaw&quot;

# Talk to the assistant (optionally deliver back to any connected channel: WhatsApp/Telegram/Slack/Discord/Google Chat/Signal/iMessage/IRC/Microsoft Teams/Matrix/Feishu/LINE/Mattermost/Nextcloud Talk/Nostr/Synology Chat/Tlon/Twitch/Zalo/Zalo Personal/WeChat/QQ/WebChat)
openclaw agent --message &quot;Ship checklist&quot; --thinking high"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Send a message</span>
openclaw message send --target +1234567890 --message <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Hello from OpenClaw<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Talk to the assistant (optionally deliver back to any connected channel: WhatsApp/Telegram/Slack/Discord/Google Chat/Signal/iMessage/IRC/Microsoft Teams/Matrix/Feishu/LINE/Mattermost/Nextcloud Talk/Nostr/Synology Chat/Tlon/Twitch/Zalo/Zalo Personal/WeChat/QQ/WebChat)</span>
openclaw agent --message <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Ship checklist<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> --thinking high</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Upgrading? <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating" rel="nofollow">Updating guide</a> (and run <code>openclaw doctor</code>).</p>
<p dir="auto">Models config + CLI: <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/models" rel="nofollow">Models</a>. Auth profile rotation + fallbacks: <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/model-failover" rel="nofollow">Model failover</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Security defaults (DM access)</h2><a id="user-content-security-defaults-dm-access" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Security defaults (DM access)" href="#security-defaults-dm-access"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">OpenClaw connects to real messaging surfaces. Treat inbound DMs as <strong>untrusted input</strong>.</p>
<p dir="auto">Full security guide: <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/security" rel="nofollow">Security</a>.
Before remote exposure, use the <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/security/exposure-runbook" rel="nofollow">Gateway exposure runbook</a>.</p>
<p dir="auto">Default behavior on Telegram/WhatsApp/Signal/iMessage/Microsoft Teams/Discord/Google Chat/Slack:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>DM pairing</strong> (<code>dmPolicy="pairing"</code> / <code>channels.discord.dmPolicy="pairing"</code> / <code>channels.slack.dmPolicy="pairing"</code>; legacy: <code>channels.discord.dm.policy</code>, <code>channels.slack.dm.policy</code>): unknown senders receive a short pairing code and the bot does not process their message.</li>
<li>Approve with: <code>openclaw pairing approve &lt;channel&gt; &lt;code&gt;</code> (then the sender is added to a local allowlist store).</li>
<li>Public inbound DMs require an explicit opt-in: set <code>dmPolicy="open"</code> and include <code>"*"</code> in the channel allowlist (<code>allowFrom</code> / <code>channels.discord.allowFrom</code> / <code>channels.slack.allowFrom</code>; legacy: <code>channels.discord.dm.allowFrom</code>, <code>channels.slack.dm.allowFrom</code>).</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Run <code>openclaw doctor</code> to surface risky/misconfigured DM policies.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Highlights</h2><a id="user-content-highlights" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Highlights" href="#highlights"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong><a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway" rel="nofollow">Local-first Gateway</a></strong> — single control plane for sessions, channels, tools, and events.</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels" rel="nofollow">Multi-channel inbox</a></strong> — WhatsApp, Telegram, Slack, Discord, Google Chat, Signal, iMessage, IRC, Microsoft Teams, Matrix, Feishu, LINE, Mattermost, Nextcloud Talk, Nostr, Synology Chat, Tlon, Twitch, Zalo, Zalo Personal, WeChat, QQ, WebChat, macOS, iOS/Android.</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/configuration" rel="nofollow">Multi-agent routing</a></strong> — route inbound channels/accounts/peers to isolated agents (workspaces + per-agent sessions).</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/nodes/voicewake" rel="nofollow">Voice Wake</a> + <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/nodes/talk" rel="nofollow">Talk Mode</a></strong> — wake words on macOS/iOS and continuous voice on Android (ElevenLabs + system TTS fallback).</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/platforms/mac/canvas" rel="nofollow">Live Canvas</a></strong> — agent-driven visual workspace with <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/platforms/mac/canvas#canvas-a2ui" rel="nofollow">A2UI</a>.</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools" rel="nofollow">First-class tools</a></strong> — browser, canvas, nodes, cron, sessions, and Discord/Slack actions.</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/platforms" rel="nofollow">Companion apps</a></strong> — Windows Hub, macOS menu bar app, and iOS/Android <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/nodes" rel="nofollow">nodes</a>.</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/start/wizard" rel="nofollow">Onboarding</a> + <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/skills" rel="nofollow">skills</a></strong> — onboarding-driven setup with bundled/managed/workspace skills.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Security model (important)</h2><a id="user-content-security-model-important" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Security model (important)" href="#security-model-important"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Default: tools run on the host for the <code>main</code> session, so the agent has full access when it is just you.</li>
<li>Group/channel safety: set <code>agents.defaults.sandbox.mode: "non-main"</code> to run non-<code>main</code> sessions inside sandboxes. Docker is the default sandbox backend; SSH and OpenShell backends are also available.</li>
<li>Typical sandbox default: allow <code>bash</code>, <code>process</code>, <code>read</code>, <code>write</code>, <code>edit</code>, <code>sessions_list</code>, <code>sessions_history</code>, <code>sessions_send</code>, <code>sessions_spawn</code>; deny <code>browser</code>, <code>canvas</code>, <code>nodes</code>, <code>cron</code>, <code>discord</code>, <code>gateway</code>.</li>
<li>Before exposing anything remotely, read <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/security" rel="nofollow">Security</a>, <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/security/exposure-runbook" rel="nofollow">Gateway exposure runbook</a>, <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/sandboxing" rel="nofollow">Sandboxing</a>, and <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/configuration" rel="nofollow">Configuration</a>.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Operator quick refs</h2><a id="user-content-operator-quick-refs" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Operator quick refs" href="#operator-quick-refs"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Chat commands: <code>/status</code>, <code>/new</code>, <code>/reset</code>, <code>/compact</code>, <code>/think &lt;level&gt;</code>, <code>/verbose on|off</code>, <code>/trace on|off</code>, <code>/usage off|tokens|full</code>, <code>/restart</code>, <code>/activation mention|always</code></li>
<li>Session tools: <code>sessions_list</code>, <code>sessions_history</code>, <code>sessions_send</code></li>
<li>Skills registry: <a href="https://clawhub.ai" rel="nofollow">ClawHub</a></li>
<li>Architecture overview: <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/architecture" rel="nofollow">Architecture</a></li>
</ul>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">From source (development)</h2><a id="user-content-from-source-development" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: From source (development)" href="#from-source-development"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Use <code>pnpm</code> for source checkouts. The repository is a pnpm workspace, and bundled
plugins load from <code>extensions/*</code> during development so their package-local
dependencies and your edits are used directly. Plain <code>npm install</code> at the repo
root is not a supported source setup.</p>
<p dir="auto">For the dev loop:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="git clone https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw.git
cd openclaw

pnpm install

# First run only (or after resetting local OpenClaw config/workspace)
pnpm openclaw setup

# Optional: prebuild Control UI before first startup
pnpm ui:build

# Dev loop (auto-reload on source/config changes)
pnpm gateway:watch"><pre>git clone https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw.git
<span class="pl-c1">cd</span> openclaw

pnpm install

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> First run only (or after resetting local OpenClaw config/workspace)</span>
pnpm openclaw setup

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Optional: prebuild Control UI before first startup</span>
pnpm ui:build

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Dev loop (auto-reload on source/config changes)</span>
pnpm gateway:watch</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">If you need a built <code>dist/</code> from the checkout (for Node, packaging, or release validation), run:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="pnpm build
pnpm ui:build"><pre>pnpm build
pnpm ui:build</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><code>pnpm openclaw setup</code> writes the local config/workspace needed for <code>pnpm gateway:watch</code>. It is safe to re-run, but you normally only need it on first setup or after resetting local state. <code>pnpm gateway:watch</code> does not rebuild <code>dist/control-ui</code>, so rerun <code>pnpm ui:build</code> after <code>ui/</code> changes or use <code>pnpm ui:dev</code> when iterating on the Control UI. If you want this checkout to run onboarding directly, use <code>pnpm openclaw onboard --install-daemon</code>.</p>
<p dir="auto">Note: <code>pnpm openclaw ...</code> runs TypeScript directly (via <code>tsx</code>). <code>pnpm build</code> produces <code>dist/</code> for running via Node / the packaged <code>openclaw</code> binary, while <code>pnpm gateway:watch</code> rebuilds the runtime on demand during the dev loop.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Development channels</h2><a id="user-content-development-channels" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Development channels" href="#development-channels"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>stable</strong>: tagged releases (<code>vYYYY.M.D</code> or <code>vYYYY.M.D-&lt;patch&gt;</code>), npm dist-tag <code>latest</code>.</li>
<li><strong>beta</strong>: prerelease tags (<code>vYYYY.M.D-beta.N</code>), npm dist-tag <code>beta</code> (macOS app may be missing).</li>
<li><strong>dev</strong>: moving head of <code>main</code>, npm dist-tag <code>dev</code> (when published).</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Switch channels (git + npm): <code>openclaw update --channel stable|beta|dev</code>.
Details: <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/development-channels" rel="nofollow">Development channels</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Agent workspace + skills</h2><a id="user-content-agent-workspace--skills" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Agent workspace + skills" href="#agent-workspace--skills"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Workspace root: <code>~/.openclaw/workspace</code> (configurable via <code>agents.defaults.workspace</code>).</li>
<li>Injected prompt files: <code>AGENTS.md</code>, <code>SOUL.md</code>, <code>TOOLS.md</code>.</li>
<li>Skills: <code>~/.openclaw/workspace/skills/&lt;skill&gt;/SKILL.md</code>.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Configuration</h2><a id="user-content-configuration" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Configuration" href="#configuration"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

  },
